# Old School RuneScape Wiki data attribution

The bundled TSV data adapts work by **Old School RuneScape Wiki contributors**, licensed under [Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial-ShareAlike 3.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-sa/3.0/). The adapted data is distributed under the same license. The Java code is licensed separately.

Modifications include extracted item identities, normalized names, combined memberships, category and role assignments, equipment and inventory ordering, variant relationships, wearing requirements, supply facts, and conditional notes. Images and article prose have been omitted. Source URLs and revision IDs within the data identify additional item-level references.
